AIRXONIX.WRP.EXE — MASTER REVERSE-ENGINEERING GUIDE
======================================================

Purpose
-------
This file consolidates the recovered architecture of AirXonix.wrp.exe so that a
human analyst or another model can start from the original EXE and reproduce the
clean-room reverse engineering without reading the historical Rxxx notes.

The native port targets the REGISTERED/FULL-GAME path. Old registration/DRM
screens, the restricted/unregistered selector and obsolete Direct3D fallback
paths are documented as non-target branches rather than silently ignored.

CURRENT r347 VALIDATION: 232/232 self-contained headless regression tests PASS
(with assertions active). Four additional clean-room/resource tests are conditional
on generated artifacts. Full EXE classification is still in progress: the direct-
call audit currently sees 692 unique internal targets and 292 are explicitly
mentioned in the present source/documentation corpus.


1. BINARY IDENTITY AND LOADING
------------------------------
Target: AirXonix.wrp.exe
Format: PE32 Windows GUI, Intel i386, 4 sections.
Linker: Microsoft linker 6.x family.
Image base: 0x00400000.
Entry point: 0x0043145B (RVA 0x0003145B).
BaseOfCode: RVA 0x00001000.
Import Address Table: RVA 0x0003B000; most direct import slots therefore live
around 0x0043B000.
Resources: RVA 0x021BA000, raw file offset 0x00046000.
Relocations are stripped. Analyze addresses as fixed VA addresses with image base
0x00400000.

Important imports: WINMM (time/joystick), DDRAW/Direct3D7, DSOUND, KERNEL32,
USER32 and standard CRT support. The native port replaces the DirectDraw/D3D7
frontend with SDL2 + OpenGL ES 2.0 but preserves the recovered CPU geometry,
lighting, timing and state behavior.

Recommended disassembler setup:
  * x86 / 32-bit / little endian.
  * PE image base 0x00400000.
  * Analyze .text normally, but do not blindly turn every byte sequence into code.
  * Treat switch tables and packed resource/data regions as data even if bytes
    decode as valid opcodes.
  * A known example is the WndProc switch data near 0x00409A88: apparent FF/call
    bytes are table contents, not a real indirect call.


2. FASTEST REVERSE-ENGINEERING WORKFLOW
---------------------------------------
1) Import the PE and preserve the fixed image base.
2) Name imported functions/IAT first. A large fraction of indirect register calls
   are aliases of IAT imports, not hidden game functions.
3) Recover DirectX COM/vtable calls separately. call [ecx+N] / call [edx+N]
   patterns are usually D3D/DirectSound dispatch.
4) Enumerate direct CALL targets inside the game range, then enumerate indirect
   call/jmp sites. Classify each as IAT, COM/vtable, switch table or true internal
   callback before declaring anything missing.
5) Search strings and FourCC constants. They quickly expose menus, SFX names,
   resource loaders and registration-only paths.
6) Trace writes to the 0x0254xxxx / 0x0257xxxx globals. These global blocks reveal
   the gameplay state machine more reliably than guessing class boundaries.
7) Reconstruct producer -> consumer pairs: model builders, prepared handles,
   update routines, then the 0x40C350 submission sites.
8) Preserve x87 conversion behavior. Do not replace every integer conversion with
   round-to-nearest; helper 0x0043129C explicitly truncates toward zero.
9) Preserve the original MSVC rand() sequence. Changing when rand() is consumed
   shifts enemy spawn, pickups, music/environment selection and presentation.
10) Build tiny regression tests for every recovered contract before moving on.


3. NUMERIC RULES THAT CAUSE EASY MISTAKES
-----------------------------------------
MSVC rand(): state starts at 1; state = state*214013 + 2531011 (32-bit wrap),
return (state >> 16) & 0x7FFF.

x87 conversion helper 0x0043129C:
  Temporarily selects truncation toward zero and performs FISTP. Any callsite that
  reaches this helper must use truncation, not lrint/lround. It matters for score,
  capture seeds, several camera angles, menu rotations and enemy angular state.

Other FISTP sites use the default x87 rounding mode and therefore correspond to
round-to-nearest. Do not globally replace all conversions with truncation.

Legacy angles use 2048 units per full revolution. Many matrix helpers operate on
this integer domain; some presentation helpers instead take radians directly.
Do not quantize a radian-only path just because a nearby path uses legacy angles.


4. CORE GAME STATE
------------------
The playfield is exactly 64x64 cells (4096 bytes).
Important low 6-bit cell states:
  0x00 = free / uncaptured.
  0x20 = safe / captured.
  0x40 = active trail.
Capture uses temporary marking plus 4-connected flood fill from airborne-enemy
seed cells; regions unreachable from enemies become captured.

Core session globals:
  0x0257D9E8  difficulty/game-time scale.
  0x0257D9EC  number of levels in selected mode.
  0x0257D9F0  score-per-captured-cell multiplier.
  0x0257D9F8  base time in seconds for level initialization.
  0x0257DA00  capture denominator.
  0x0257DA04  initial occupied-cell count at level entry.
  0x0257DA08  previous/current occupied-cell snapshot used for delta progress.
  0x0257DA10  lives.
  0x0257DA14  time remaining in milliseconds.
  0x0257DA18  current level number, 1-based inside level gameplay.
  0x0257DA1C  captured percent. It may exceed 100; do NOT clamp before scoring/UI.
  0x0257DA20  score.
  0x0257DA24  bonus accumulator.
  0x0257DA40  current Xonix speed.
  0x0257DA48/4C/50  selected source position used by effects/audio paths.
  0x0257DA58/5C/60  camera position X/Y/Z.
  0x0257DA68/6C/70  camera legacy angles 1/2/3.
  0x0257DA74  active-trail flag.
  0x0257DA7C  scaled/smoothed legacy dt output.
  0x0257DADC  current max-speed/effect target.
  0x0053B684  global brightness/light multiplier; NOT camera distance.

Level/session control:
  0x00418DD0  per-level initialization.
  0x004194D0  occupied-cell/capture/score progress update.
  0x004195D0  main gameplay session body.
  0x00424DE0  outer campaign/gameplay wrapper.

Player movement:
  The Xonix has separate current speed and max-speed/effect target. Base max speed
  is 0.03, Slow is 0.012, Fast is 0.043. The pickup changes the LIMIT first;
  current speed is clamped later by the crossing/update logic, not immediately.
  The current speed survives death and level transitions; session bootstrap is
  the point that initializes it to the base value.

A no-movement/blocked cell crossing reaches the common tail around 0x0041A057 and
sets the remaining crossing progress to 0. This is why low-speed input remains
responsive; leaving progress at 1 creates multi-second stalls.

Restart/Backspace:
  Score and lives are snapshotted on level entry. Restart restores those entry
  values, not values from the moment Backspace is pressed. Five restart credits
  are separate from Xonix lives.


5. CAPTURE, SCORE AND COMPLETION
--------------------------------
0x004194D0 walks all 4096 field bytes, masks with 0x3F and counts occupied cells.
Captured percent is based on newly occupied cells relative to the initial count
and the level denominator. Values above 100 are valid and feed over-capture bonus.

Score conversion uses 0x0043129C truncation-toward-zero, not nearest rounding.

0x0041A980 — normal level-complete scoring / inter-level entry.
0x0041B2A0 — finale scoring prologue.
Completion adds the remaining-time bonus and over-capture bonus. Finale then adds
life and mode-completion bonuses as separate float additions/conversions; preserve
that order because float32 + truncation can differ by one point from an algebraic
combined expression.


6. ENTITIES AND SPECIAL OBJECTS
-------------------------------
Model/update families:
  0x00420660  Xonix body + repeated rotor model construction.
  0x00420C10  crawler/ground-enemy model.
  0x00420F40  homing special model.
  0x00421190  field eraser model.
  0x004213A0  active-trail normal/damaged variants.
  0x00421540  four airborne enemy subtype meshes.
  0x00421730  six normal pickup meshes.
  0x00422240  six auxiliary event/effect meshes.
  0x004223E0  six cinematic/sequence meshes.
  0x00422D20  14-segment airborne shadow/decal.

Auxiliary effects:
  0x004156A0 init, 0x004156C0 spawn, 0x00415880 update/presentation.
  Runtime base 0x0257DB00.
  Models correspond to BONU/TIME/LIFE/SLOW/ACCE/TOU2 and are rendered through
  texture atlas 7. Their plaque pitch is billboarded from camera Y/Z.

Particles:
  0x004175B0 spawn, 0x00417700 update, 0x0040E120 render frontend.
  Pool/base metadata is mirrored by the native trace structs.

Death/crawler impact overlay:
  0x0041BDB0 starts the four-vertex additive shockwave/flash.
  0x0041BEE0 updates/renders it using ONE/ONE blending.
  Crawler impact is orange (255,50,0); ground impact in the death trajectory is
  cyan (0,255,255). It begins at +/-0.01, expands by 0.00015*dt and fades at
  0.003*dt. UV rectangle is the recovered 193..255 x 161..223 atlas region.


7. RENDERER AND CAMERA
----------------------
Master vertex pipeline:
  0x0040C190  prepare/set RGB light multipliers.
  0x0040C250  camera basis setup.
  0x0040C350  CPU transform/clip/project/light frontend and mesh submit.
The output corresponds to a 32-byte D3DTLVERTEX-style record:
  sx, sy, sz, rhw, diffuse, specular, u, v.

Camera uses a 2048-entry trig/angle system, stored position and a 3x3 orientation
basis. The native GLES renderer deliberately preserves this CPU frontend rather
than replacing it with a generic modern lookAt/projection approximation.

Procedural geometry:
  0x00401750  adaptive rounded/full sphere builder.
  0x004020D0  adaptive hemisphere builder; scratch vertex + triangle-only faces.
  0x00402510  extruded contour family.
  0x004032A0 / 0x00403580  circular cap/fan family.
  0x00403400 / 0x004036E0  annular/ribbon family.
  0x004041A0  flat frame.
  0x00404620  thin beveled rectangular/octagonal prism: 32 vertices, 14 quads.
              All 16 side vertices use the same (u0,v0) sample; do not invent a
              perimeter UV unwrap.

Reflections use the recovered reflection transform path rather than duplicate
static models; reflected crawlers/pickups/menu objects are separate draw submits.


8. LEVEL INTRO / GAME ENTRY
---------------------------
0x0041CEA0 is the startup/level-entry presentation wrapper. It is not a single
3-second sleep. The recovered registered/capable path is:

A) 3-second LevelIntro backend 0x0041D4D0.
   - remaining time begins at 3000 ms.
   - choreography changes at remaining <= 1000 ms.
   - camera angle2 starts at -450.
   - Xonix presentation starts near (0.5015625,0.32,0.3921875).
   - world is live enough to update airborne/pickups/background according to the
     recovered call ordering, while gameplay input/timer are blocked.
   - LEV2 plaque and dynamic CNT3 digits are drawn as separate passes.
   - 0x0040EE40 builds the CNT3 decimal digit mesh (4 vertices/1 quad per digit,
     max six digits, exact recovered UV stepping).

B) Live-world fade-in/entry scene.
   - brightness rises toward 255 at dt*0.17241378.
   - camera approaches gameplay state; angle2 moves toward -302.
   - Xonix descends toward Y=0.008 with decreasing vertical speed.
   - retained SFX 7 is active; cleanup stops it and emits spatial SFX 9.
   - optional speech alternates logical cues 41/42 using a persistent selector.
   - homing, eraser, airborne, pickups, background and progress update occur in
     the recovered order before normal gameplay begins.

Environment theme selection occurs BEFORE per-level entity/pickup RNG; gameplay
music selection occurs only after the 3-second intro. Consuming both rand() calls
at level-load start shifts the complete RNG stream and is incorrect.

The D3D capability fallback backend is an obsolete renderer compatibility branch.
GLES2 always satisfies the required blending capability, so the native target
implements the capable/full path.


9. HUD / TEXT / MENUS
---------------------
0x004247E0 is the HUD update/render frontend.
Score and timer have separate DISPLAY counters that chase the actual values at
roughly dt*32 instead of snapping every frame. The smoother is applied after the
current frame's gameplay state update. Low time (<=10 s) and last life (<=1)
produce the recovered pulse color.

Captured percent is not clamped to 100. Values >99 use three digits.

Text-grid fade uses a 32-level palette law:
  component_out = floor(component * level / 31), level 0..31.
Do not approximate this with a 0..255 float multiplier; Controls/Records/
Information/ModeSelect all rely on the byte-domain behavior.

Important screens:
  0x004112F0  restricted/unregistered Mode Select (DRM path; non-target).
  0x00411870  registered/full Mode Select used by the port.
  0x0040FF70  license agreement violation screen (registration/anti-tamper only).
  0x0041DE30  Pause modal.
  0x0041E520  Abort modal.

Registered Mode Select:
  - title CP1251 text: " ВЫБЕРИТЕ ИГРУ: ".
  - rows show level count followed by "Этапов"; selected row white, others
    0x008FFF before palette fade.
  - M101/slot0 + airborne subtype 0 + crawler pair + reflections are actual 3D
    scene submits, not just a 2D list.
  - footer row 14 uses a red pulse:
      trunc((sin(angle)+1)*31 + 128) << 16.
  - full text grid uses fade level = fadeCounter >> 6.

Records:
  - mode names come from SOUNDINF, not hardcoded English/native labels.
  - heading "Р Е К О Р Д Ы" has the recovered moving wave color.
  - name-entry prompt is "ВВЕДИТЕ ВАШЕ ИМЯ" with its grayscale pulse.
  - whole grid uses the same 32-level fade law.

Information:
  - all three pages fade through the same palette law.
  - footer pulse modulates RED only.
  - footer pulse phase resets per page.
  - Xonix/sway float phases are not all wrapped; preserve each callsite's exact
    wrap/no-wrap behavior.

Settings/Controls:
  - Settings fade counter grows by dt*7 to 0x7C0 and blocks input until ready.
  - exit/Controls transitions perform the recovered fade-out/fade-in sequences.
  - Speech toggles with Left/Right/Action and click SFX.
  - Up/Down navigation uses logical SFX 0x15.
  - Controls text uses the exact palette-level fade, not counter>>3/255.

Pause/Abort lighting:
  Pause world tint is (gray,gray,255), where gray = 165 - panelY*300.
  Abort uses its separate green-family curves; Yes and No/Escape follow different
  color branches. Keep RGB argument order exactly as passed to 0x40C190.


10. AUDIO
---------
Legacy output format built around 0x00409F51..0x00409F9B:
  PCM, stereo, 22050 Hz, 8-bit, blockAlign 2, 44100 bytes/sec.

SFX:
  0x00423140  initialize 64 logical FourCC slots.
  0x0040AE00  resolve FourCC table.
  0x0040AE50  play simple SFX.
  0x0040AE90  play spatial SFX.
  0x0040AEF0  allocate one of seven 32-byte SFX voices.
  0x0040AF70  update live spatial voice.
  0x0040AFA0  stop/clear voice.
  0x0040A890  software mixer.
  0x0040A7B0  spatial left/right gain calculation.
  Voice pool: 0x00451548, 7 slots, stride 0x20.
  Listener position: 0x00451534/38/3C.

Gain lookup:
  0x00409DF0 builds the 96x256 signed contribution table at 0x00457A54.
  Contribution is effectively ((sample-128)*gain)/64 with truncation toward zero.
  Spatial channel gain is capped at 0x55 (85).

Music:
  0x0040AFC0  map/open music\\NN.mus.
  0x0040B0D0  stream/fade update.
  0x0040B240  request track.
  0x0040B260  begin fade-out.
  0x00422EA0  reset gameplay music usage counters.
  0x00422EE0  least-used/random track selector for tracks 0..9.
  Usage counters: 0x025B78A4.
  `music\\29.mus` is not normal BGM; it is the raw mono SFX sample bank.

The music selector deliberately uses `(rand() & 0x15) + 1` while cyclically
counting minimum-use entries. It is NOT modulo 10.


11. ORIGINAL RESOURCE CONTAINER
-------------------------------
AirXonix.wrp.exe can be used directly as a fidelity resource container.
Recovered fixed locations:
  PE .rsrc raw file offset = 0x00046000.
  .rsrc RVA               = 0x021BA000.
  BMPPACK RVA             = 0x021BA570.
  BMPPACK bytes           = 0x001157E0.
  SOUNDINF RVA            = 0x022CFD50.
  SOUNDINF bytes          = 0x00001CA4.
  WAVEPACK RVA            = 0x022D19F4.
  WAVEPACK bytes          = 0x000001A0.

The nine original texture atlases are RGB565: three 64x64, one 128x128 and five
256x256 atlases. Runtime upload records map FourCC -> atlas/x/y directly; there
is no hidden color or UV remapping stage.

SOUNDINF supplies five mode headers/names and the recovered level records. The
native port supports all 82 reachable level records. Tail/padding records that
are not reachable through the original mode counts are not gameplay content.


12. REGISTERED VS DRM/UNREGISTERED PATHS
-----------------------------------------
The original program contains old commercial registration checks. Do not confuse
these with missing gameplay:
  * 0x004112F0 restricted/unregistered selector is not the normal full-game UI.
  * 0x0040FF70 license-agreement-violation screen belongs to anti-tamper logic.
  * Several pointer-derived globals around early bootstrap are integrity/protection
    plumbing rather than gameplay counters.
The PortMaster clean-room runtime intentionally follows the registered/full-game
branch and omits the DRM user experience.


13. HOW TO VERIFY A NEW DECOMPILATION AGAINST THIS MAP
------------------------------------------------------
For each recovered function:
  A. Confirm incoming callsites and outgoing calls.
  B. Record every global read/write address.
  C. Separate data tables from code before naming indirect calls.
  D. Recover constants as exact float32 values from the binary, not decimal guesses.
  E. Identify whether float->int uses normal FISTP or 0x43129C trunc-helper.
  F. Record RNG consumption order.
  G. Record renderer state (texture, blending, Z test/write, lighting) separately
     from geometry transforms.
  H. Build a minimal deterministic regression reproducing the observed contract.

The source tree mirrors this approach with `legacy_*_trace.hpp` structures and
hundreds of small tests. Those files are the executable specification of the map.

which category an entry belongs to; do not assume every address is a function.
